We attribute this ambiguity to identity-slot permutation freedom: without explicit identity-slot alignment, the correspondence between semantic parts and generation slots is not identifiable during training, allowing multiple slot assignments to fit the same supervision and leading to inconsistent decomposition. Based on this insight, we argue that stable part-aware generation requires identity-aligned one-to-one slot modelling. We therefore propose an identity-slot aligned framework, ISAP-3D, which anchors each part with semantic identity tokens and performs identity-conditioned one-to-one layout prediction, followed by layout-conditioned geometry synthesis. Structured local-global conditioning maintains identity alignment across semantic, spatial, and geometric stages. We also construct a part-level dataset with a unified semantic protocol to enable learnable and consistent identity-slot alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved structural stability, controllability, and robustness over state-of-the-art part-aware generation baselines.

</details>


### [78] [MSUE: Multi-Modal Soccer Understanding Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12106)
*Litao Li,Yibo Yu,Yufeng Hu,Zhuo Yang,Jiali Wen,Yixin Chen,Yixi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向2026 SoccerNet VQA挑战赛的解决方案，包含数据合成流程和MSUE多专家问答架构，最终以0.95准确率获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决体育视频领域视觉问答任务中标注数据稀缺、单一模型能力不足的问题，以低成本生成高质量训练数据并提升VQA性能。

Method: 1) 基于VLM的数据合成管道，将原始领域数据重构为多样化VQA样本；2) MSUE多专家架构，由LLM动态调度文本专家(Gemini3-Flash)、图像专家(微调Qwen3-VL)和视频专家(外部知识库)协同回答。

Result: 在挑战赛基准上达到0.95的准确率，位列排行榜第三名。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据合成方法与多专家协作架构在体育视频VQA任务上取得优异表现，验证了低成本数据增强和专家协同机制的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents our solution to the 2026 SoccerNet VQA Challenge. We first develop a cost-effective data synthesis pipeline driven by a Vision-Language Model (VLM), which systematically restructures raw domain data into diverse VQA samples, including concise answers and long-form responses. Second, we propose MSUE, a multi-expert question answering architecture that employs a Large Language Model (LLM) to dynamically dispatch questions to text, image, and video experts. These experts are instantiated as a strong text baseline Gemini3-Flash, a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL, and an external knowledge base, respectively, working collaboratively to enhance VQA performance. MSUE achieves an accuracy of \textbf{0.95} on the challenge benchmark, securing third place in the leaderboard.

</details>


### [79] [Q-Fold: Query-Aware Focus-Context Spatio-Temporal Folding for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12125)
*Biao Tang,Xu Chen,Shuxiang Gou,Jingyi Yuan,Yuhan Zhang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Q-Fold是一种无需训练的输入构建框架，通过查询引导的异构Focus-Context表示，将长视频分割为时间连续的片段，保留关键帧和上下文布局，在不增加输入预算的情况下显著提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在有限视觉预算下处理长视频时仍遵循以帧为中心的模式，对保留内容采用相似表示，难以同时兼顾高保真视觉证据和广泛的时间覆盖。

Method: 提出Q-Fold框架，以连续时间段而非孤立帧为基本单元，在查询引导下构建异构的Focus-Context表示：相关段保留为高保真焦点帧，不相关段折叠为保持时间顺序的上下文布局。

Result: 在四个长视频基准测试和多个Video-MLLM上，Q-Fold在输入预算不变的情况下持续提升性能，在超长视频基准上最高提升9.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-Fold通过异构表示和查询引导策略，在不增加计算成本的前提下，有效兼顾了关键视觉证据保留和广泛时间覆盖，为长视频理解提供了一种简洁高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-video understanding remains challenging for multimodal large language models, because temporally extended videos often contain thousands of frames and are therefore expensive to process exhaustively. Existing methods usually construct compact visual inputs from long videos under a limited visual budget. However, most of them still follow a frame-centric paradigm and apply similar representations to retained content regardless of its importance. This makes it difficult to preserve both high-fidelity visual evidence and broad temporal coverage. To address this issue, we propose Q-Fold, a training-free input construction framework for long-video understanding. Instead of treating isolated frames as the basic modeling unit, Q-Fold operates on contiguous temporal segments and constructs a heterogeneous Focus--Context representation under query guidance. Query-relevant segments are preserved as high-fidelity Focus Frames, while less relevant segments are folded into chronology-preserving contextual layouts. In this way, Q-Fold preserves critical visual evidence and broad temporal coverage, while better maintaining local temporal continuity within short segments. Experiments on four long-video benchmarks with multiple Video-MLLMs show that Q-Fold consistently improves performance without increasing the input budget. Notably, it achieves gains of up to 9.1 percentage points on an ultra-long video benchmark. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [80] [AGE-MIL: Anchor-Guided Evidence Learning for Patient-Level Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12126)
*Jiawei Niu,Jian Chen,Di Zhang,Junbo Lu,Zhangcheng Liao,Xuhao Liu,Honglin Zhong,Mireia Crispin-Ortuzar,Chen Li,Zeyu Gao,Yi Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGE-MIL提出了一种基于锚点引导的证据多实例学习框架，通过构建患者级锚点整合多张全切片图像信息，实现弱监督下的患者级预测，在六个临床任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理方法主要基于单张WSI的MIL范式，但临床实践中病理学家需要整合多张WSI进行诊断。直接对传统MIL框架施加患者级监督会导致优化不稳定和预测可靠性下降。

Method: AGE-MIL框架包含三个关键组件：1) 构建患者级锚点，捕获全局病理上下文；2) 通过锚点引导检索和整合诊断相关的局部补丁；3) 将患者级风险建模为证据累积过程，在弱监督下实现稳定优化。

Result: 在两个独立数据集的六个临床相关患者级预测任务上，AGE-MIL一致优于八种主流MIL方法。

Conclusion: AGE-MIL通过患者级锚点整合多切片信息，有效解决了传统MIL框架在患者级预测中的不稳定性问题，为计算病理学中的患者级建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing computational pathology methods predominantly operate within whole-slide image (WSI)-level multiple instance learning (MIL) paradigms, while patient-level modeling remains underexplored. In routine pathological practice, however, pathologists derive diagnostic and prognostic conclusions by integrating evidence across multiple WSIs rather than relying on any single slide. This discrepancy creates a fundamental misalignment when patient-level supervision is directly imposed on conventional MIL frameworks, often leading to unstable optimization and degraded predictive reliability. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-Guided Evidence MIL (AGE-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level prediction. AGE-MIL constructs a patient-level anchor from slide representations to capture global pathological context and guide the retrieval and integration of diagnostically relevant local patches, enabling robust patient-level modeling. Patient-level risk is further modeled as an evidence accumulation process, promoting stable optimization under weak supervision. AGE-MIL is evaluated on six clinically relevant patient-level prediction tasks from two independent cohorts. Experimental results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art MIL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wodeniua/AGE-MIL.

</details>


### [81] [Time-Conditioned and Multi-Time Survival Prediction from 2D PET/CT Projections in Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12140)
*Ashish Chauhan,Sambit Tarai,Elin Lundström,Johan Öfverstedt,Håkan Ahlström,Joel Kullberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间建模对PET/CT影像生存预测的影响，提出两种新方法（ATCS和MTS），在848例NSCLC患者数据上验证，均优于基线模型，并发现不同时间粒度对短/长期预测有不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间建模对基于影像的生存预测影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ATCS（注意力引导时间条件生存）和MTS（多时间生存）两种方法，以TCS为基线，在848例NSCLC患者PET/CT数据上训练，5折交叉验证，采用时间依赖AUC评估0.5-5年预测性能。

Result: ATCS（0.794）和MTS（0.793）的平均AUC均优于基线TCS（0.767）；ATCS在早期（0.5-3年）更优，MTS在后期（3.5-5年）更优；肿瘤特异+组织特征融合优于单一输入；更细时间离散化提升短期预测，更粗粒度提供稳定长期估计。

Conclusion: 时间建模和输入设计影响PET/CT生存预测，所提方法可实现治疗前影像的时间特异性生存估计，有助于改进风险分层和临床决策。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of overall survival (OS) from positron emission tomography/computed tomography (PET/CT) can support personalized treatment and follow-up strategies in oncology. However, the impact of temporal modeling on imaging-based survival prediction remains insufficiently explored. We investigate how different temporal formulations influence survival prediction by developing two complementary approaches: Attention-guided Time-Conditioned Survival (ATCS) and Multi-Time Survival (MTS). We retrospectively analyzed pre-treatment PET/CT images from 848 patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), including 556 for model development and 292 for held-out testing. A previously proposed Time-Conditioned Survival (TCS) model was used as a baseline. Models were trained using 5-fold cross-validation and evaluated on the test set using time-dependent area under the curve (AUC) at 6-month intervals from 0.5 to 5 years. Both ATCS and MTS outperformed the baseline TCS model, achieving mean AUCs of 0.794 and 0.793, respectively, compared to 0.767. ATCS performed better at earlier time points (0.5-3 years), whereas MTS performed better at later intervals (3.5-5 years). Combining tumor-specific and tissue-wise PET/CT features improved performance over either input alone. Finer temporal discretization improved short-term prediction, while coarser intervals provided more stable long-term estimates. These findings demonstrate that temporal modeling and input design influence PET/CT-based survival prediction. The proposed approaches enable time-specific survival estimation from pre-treatment imaging and may support improved risk stratification and clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [82] [TopoCap: Learning Topology-Agnostic Motion Priors for Monocular Video-to-Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12153)
*Cheng-Feng Pu,Jia-Peng Zhang,Meng-Hao Guo,Yan-Pei Cao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoCap是一个统一的运动捕捉与重定向框架，能从单目视频中提取动作并迁移到任意骨骼拓扑结构的3D角色上，无需测试时优化。核心是通过图CVAE学习通用运动流形，将运动动力学与骨骼拓扑解耦，并在大规模多样化数据集Mobjaverse上训练，实现对人类、四足动物及长尾生物的零样本重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作捕捉方法受限于物种特定的骨骼模板（如SMPL）或需要繁琐的手动骨骼绑定，无法泛化到任意拓扑结构（从双足到六足甚至无生命物体），亟需统一的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段生成管线：1）使用图CVAE学习通用运动流形，将异构运动链压缩为固定长度潜码，并通过目标骨架的结构嵌入显式解耦运动与拓扑；2）将视频到动画视为条件流匹配问题，从视觉特征预测拓扑无关的潜码。

Result: 在人类和四足动物基准上超越专业模型，同时实现对长尾3D生物的零样本重定向。构建了Mobjaverse数据集，包含5,000+独特骨骼拓扑和200万帧。

Conclusion: TopoCap证明了运动动力学存在于连续低维流形中，可通过拓扑无关的表征实现跨物种动作迁移，为大规模3D资产动画提供了统一范式。

Abstract: The explosion of generative 3D assets has created a massive demand for animation, yet current motion capture methods remain brittle, restricted to species-specific templates (e.g., SMPL) or requiring labor-intensive manual rigging. We introduce TopoCap, the first unified framework capable of extracting motion from monocular video and retargeting it onto characters with arbitrary, unseen skeletal topologies, i.e., from bipeds to hexapods and inanimate objects, without test-time optimization. Our key insight is that while skeletal structures are combinatorial and discrete, the underlying physics of motion occupy a continuous, low-dimensional manifold. We materialize this insight via a two-stage generative pipeline. First, we learn a Universal Motion Manifold using a Graph CVAE that compresses heterogeneous kinematic chains into a shared, fixed-length latent code. By explicitly conditioning the decoder on a structural embedding of the target rig, we disentangle motion dynamics from skeletal topology. Second, we treat video-to-animation as a conditional flow matching problem, predicting these topology-agnostic codes from visual features. To learn this generalized prior, we introduce Mobjaverse, a massive-scale dataset curated from Objaverse-XL. Comprising over 5,000 unique skeletal topologies and 2 million frames, it exceeds the structural diversity of existing datasets by two orders of magnitude. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \MethodMotion outperforms specialist models on human and quadruped benchmarks while enabling zero-shot retargeting for the long tail of 3D creatures. Dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/duckduckplz/Mobjaverse.

</details>


### [83] [OpenMedReason: Scientific Reasoning Supervision for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12169)
*Negin Baghbanzadeh,Pritam Sarkar,Michael Colacci,Abeer Badawi,Adibvafa Fallahpour,Arash Afkanpour,Leonid Sigal,Ali Etemad,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenMedReason是一个包含约45万图像-问题-答案实例的大规模开放式医学多模态推理语料库，其推理轨迹源自真实生物医学科学文献。配套的OpenMedReason-Bench基准可从感知、医学知识和推理三个维度细粒度评估LVLM。训练后VQA准确率平均提升20%，推理轨迹在86.1%的成对比较中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险临床场景下，大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）需要基于视觉证据和临床知识进行推理，而不仅仅是给出正确答案。现有数据集缺乏高质量的、可解释的推理轨迹监督信号。

Method: 从策划的生物医学科学文献中提取推理轨迹，构建约45万实例的多模态医学推理语料库OpenMedReason，涵盖放射扫描、显微图像、可见光照片、图表等多种医学视觉模态，并设计三轴细粒度评估基准OpenMedReason-Bench。

Result: 在监督微调和基于强化学习的对齐中均有效，VQA准确率平均提升20%，性能达到最强可比规模医学LVLM的4.2%以内。感知、医学知识和推理三个维度均得到提升，推理轨迹在86.1%的成对比较中优于基线模型。

Conclusion: OpenMedReason作为丰富的训练资源，能有效提升LVLM在多模态医学推理任务中的综合表现，且改进是全面而非单维度的。

Abstract: High-stakes clinical use of large vision-language models (LVLMs) requires reasoning that is grounded in visual evidence and clinical knowledge, not just correct final answers. We introduce OpenMedReason, a large-scale, open multimodal medical reasoning corpus comprising approximately 450K image-question-answer instances whose reasoning traces are primarily derived from curated biomedical, human-authored scientific articles. OpenMedReason provides high-fidelity supervision beyond synthetic chains of thought, covering diverse medical domain vision modalities such as radiological scans, microscopic images, visible light photographs, charts, and others. We complement it with OpenMedReason-Bench, a held-out benchmark that allows fine-grained evaluation of LVLMs along three complementary axes of capability, including perception, medical knowledge, and rationale, enabling diagnostic evaluation beyond final-answer accuracy. OpenMedReason is a rich training resource that exhibits its effectiveness in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement-based alignment. Training with OpenMedReason yields a 20% average improvement in VQA accuracy over the base model and achieves performance within 4.2% of the strongest comparable-scale medical LVLMs. Fine-grained performance analysis confirms that the gains are not concentrated in any single axis: OpenMedReason improves perception, medical knowledge, and rationale jointly, and its reasoning traces are preferred over those of the base model in 86.1% of pairwise comparisons. We release the code and dataset at huggingface.co/datasets/neginb/OpenMedReason.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Dark Knowledge: Mixup-Based Distillation for Reliable Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12171)
*José Medina,Paul Honeine,Abdelaziz Bensrhair,Amnir Hadachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识蒸馏中教师模型在未见过的邻域分布上提供监督信号时，学生模型不仅能超越“暗知识”传递，还能独立获得更强的线性结构特性，同时温度缩放可调节精度-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对知识蒸馏与mixup的交互机制理解不足，特别是mixup仅用于学生训练时，教师被查询其未训练过的邻域分布，这种受控不匹配对知识传递的影响尚未被刻画。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示教师监督信号被分布混淆主导而非类别间结构，并在CIFAR和ImageNet上对不同容量师生模型进行实验，比较标准KD与mixup蒸馏的性能差异。

Result: mixup蒸馏持续提升学生准确率，并将过度自信降低一个数量级；校准能力独立于准确率传递；温度缩放控制可测量的精度-校准权衡，且在邻域训练下更加显著。

Conclusion: mixup蒸馏并非标准KD的劣化版本，而是一种更丰富的传递通道，同时塑造判别性能、不确定性估计和表征几何。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) and mixup have proven effective at inducing smoothness in class boundaries; KD captures inherent class relationships in probability distributions, and mixup enforces them through convex combinations of inputs. Their interaction, however, remains poorly understood, particularly when mixup is applied only during student training. In this setting, the teacher is queried on inputs drawn from a vicinal distribution it never saw during training, a controlled mismatch whose effect on knowledge transfer has not been characterised. We show that this mismatch causes the teacher's supervisory signal to be dominated by distributional confusion rather than inter-class structure. Despite it, the student does not merely imitate the teacher: it independently acquires greater linearity in the vicinal region, a structural property that the teacher lacks, and goes beyond dark-knowledge transfer. KD with mixup consistently improves student accuracy and reduces overconfidence by an order of magnitude relative to the baseline, across CIFAR and ImageNet with varying-capacity teachers. Crucially, calibration propagates from teacher to student independently of accuracy transfer, and temperature scaling governs a measurable accuracy-calibration trade-off that becomes more pronounced under vicinal training. These results reframe mixup distillation not as a degraded version of standard KD, but as a richer transfer channel that simultaneously shapes discriminative performance, uncertainty estimation, and representational geometry.

</details>


### [85] [DynaTok: Token-Based 4D Reconstruction from Partial Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12189)
*Weirong Chen,Keisuke Tateno,Hidenobu Matsuki,Michael Niemeyer,Daniel Cremers,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaTok是一种基于点云的4D重建框架，能从部分点云序列中无对应地重建完整且时序一致的4D点云，无需图像输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多依赖图像，而基于点云的方法通常需要完整输入或显式对应关系，难以处理深度传感器观测中常见的部分、无序、缺乏时序对应的问题。

Method: 将帧编码为紧凑潜变量令牌，通过Transformer时空编码器聚合时序信息，利用残差令牌解耦几何与运动，最后用流匹配解码器重建完整、时序一致的4D点云序列。

Result: 在物体级和场景级基准上，从部分点云观测中实现了更优的重建质量和时序一致性。

Conclusion: DynaTok在无图像、无显式对应关系的纯几何条件下，有效实现了从部分点云序列到完整4D重建。

Abstract: We address 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences, where depth-sensor observations are incomplete, unordered, and lack explicit temporal correspondences. This geometry-only setting is challenging due to missing observations and ambiguous dynamics. While recent progress has largely relied on image-based methods, existing point-based approaches typically focus on single objects, assume relatively complete inputs, or require explicit correspondences. To address these limitations, we propose DynaTok, a point-based framework for correspondence-free 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences without images. DynaTok encodes frames into compact latent tokens, aggregates incomplete observations over time with a Transformer-based spatiotemporal encoder, and decouples geometry and motion through residual tokens in a unified model. A flow-matching decoder then reconstructs complete, temporally consistent 4D point-cloud sequences conditioned on the latent tokens. Experiments on object- and scene-level benchmarks demonstrate improved reconstruction quality and temporal coherence from partial point cloud observations. Project page: https://wrchen530.github.io/dynatok/.

</details>


### [86] [InternVideo3: Agentify Foundation Models with Multimodal Contextual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12195)
*Ziang Yan,Sheng Xia,Jiashuo Yu,Yue Wu,Tianxiang Jiang,Songze Li,Kanghui Tian,Yicheng Xu,Yinan He,Kai Chen,Limin Wang,Yu Qiao,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InternVideo3通过多模态上下文推理（MCR）和多模态多头潜在注意力（M^2LA）机制，提升基础模型在长时序多模态视频任务中的智能体能力，在Video-MME、MLVU、EgoSchema等基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源基础模型主要聚焦文本场景，长时序多模态任务（如视频理解）中需要持续时间理解和迭代交互的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出多模态上下文推理（MCR），将理解视为闭环过程，通过共享演化上下文（包含观察、指令、推理、工具动作和记忆）进行证据积累与验证；引入多模态多头潜在注意力（M^2LA），通过保持完整token流的KV缓存重参数化提升效率；采用分阶段训练（持续预训练、短到长监督微调、基于规则的强化学习、在线策略蒸馏）。

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU、EgoSchema等视频理解基准上取得强性能，并作为带检索工具的视频智能体展现出基于证据的稳健行为。

Conclusion: 高效的上下文处理和闭环推理对于将开放多模态模型适配到长时序视觉智能体任务至关重要。

Abstract: Recent progress in foundation models has shifted toward agentic behavior involving multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, open-source efforts largely focus on text-dominant settings, leaving long-horizon multimodal tasks underexplored. This gap is evident in video tasks requiring sustained temporal understanding and iterative interaction. We present InternVideo3, a framework enhancing these capabilities via Multimodal Contextual Reasoning (MCR). MCR treats understanding as a closed-loop process over a shared, evolving context containing observations, instructions, reasoning, tool actions, and memory. This frames long-video understanding as evidence accumulation and verification. To ensure efficiency, we introduce Multimodal Multi-head Latent Attention (M^2LA), a token-preserving reparameterization compressing KV-cache states while retaining the full token stream. Our staged training includes continued pretraining, short-to-long supervised fine-tuning, rule-based reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation. Experiments show InternVideo3 achieves strong performance on benchmarks like Video-MME, MLVU, and EgoSchema. We further instantiate the model as a video agent with retrieval tools, demonstrating robust evidence-grounded behavior. Our results suggest that efficient context handling and closed-loop reasoning are vital for adapting open multimodal models toward long-horizon visually grounded agency.

</details>


### [87] [SHERPA: Seam-aware Harmonized ERP Adaptation for Open-Domain 360$^\circ$ Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12213)
*Jungwoon Kang,Jaehun Kim,Yiwon Yu,Hyungyum Jang,Sanghoon Lee,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHERPA是一个轻量级适配框架，通过频率选择性Circular RoPE、循环潜编码/解码、图像侧FFN适配器和双路径训练方案，使平面图像训练的扩散模型能够生成360°全景图像，支持真实感和风格化两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像在游戏、仿真等领域需求日益增长，但现有文本到图像的扩散模型基于平面图像训练，与360°全景的环绕拓扑和极地区域存在几何不对齐问题，需要通过轻量级适配来解决。

Method: 提出SHERPA框架，包含四个关键组件：1) 频率选择性Circular RoPE（仅替换高频水平RoPE带为整数周期谐波）；2) 循环潜编码/解码；3) 图像侧FFN适配器；4) 双路径训练方案（配对全景路径监督几何，非配对风格路径通过自监督偏航一致性实现无目标风格提示）。

Result: SHERPA能够在真实感全景域和开放域风格化提示下生成360°全景图像。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了平面预训练模型与全景拓扑之间的不对齐问题，实现了高质量的全景图像生成。

Abstract: Panoramic imagery is increasingly used in world-generation, games, and simulation, where users may need not only photorealistic scenes but also stylized and non-photorealistic environments. Large-scale text-to-image diffusion and flow models provide broad style and semantic priors for this goal, but planar image training misaligns them with the wrap-around topology and polar regions of $360^\circ$ panoramas represented in equirectangular projection (ERP). We present SHERPA, a lightweight adaptation framework that combines frequency-selective Circular RoPE, Circular Latent Encoding/Decoding, image-side FFN adapters, and a Dual-Path Training Scheme. Circular RoPE replaces only the seam-sensitive high-frequency horizontal RoPE band with integer-periodic harmonics while preserving the pretrained lower-frequency spectrum. The Paired Panorama Path supervises geometry, while the Unpaired Style Path uses self-supervised yaw consistency for target-free stylized prompts. As a result, SHERPA generates $360^\circ$ panoramas across both photorealistic panorama domains and open-domain stylized prompts.

</details>


### [88] [MLT-Dedup: Efficient Large-Scale Online Video Deduplication via Multi-Level Representations and Spatial-Temporal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12215)
*David Yuchen Wang,Haoying Li,Hailun Xu,Wei Chee Yew,Zirui Zhu,Sanjay Saha,Hao Hei,Kanchan Sarkar,Kun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MLT-Dedup，一个面向大规模在线视频去重的多层级表示与时序匹配框架，通过多层视频编码器提取粗细粒度特征，结合差分增强相似度模块实现高效精准的候选检索与配对匹配，在实际平台中重复率降低91%，精度达90%，索引容量提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台中海量用户生成视频存在大量近重复内容，影响用户体验并增加存储和带宽成本。现有去重框架在有限索引预算下难以检索到足够高质量候选，且效率与精度之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出MLT-Dedup框架，包含：1) 多层级视频编码器(ML-VE)，同时提取细粒度帧级嵌入和稀疏片段级嵌入；2) 差分增强相似度模块(DiF-SiM)，用于定位重复时序段并提供可靠相似性证据；3) 稀疏检索支持高效候选检索，细粒度嵌入用于精确配对匹配。

Result: 在真实大规模平台上，MLT-Dedup以90%精度将在线重复率降低91%，稀疏检索设计实现5倍索引容量提升。

Conclusion: MLT-Dedup通过多层级表示和时空匹配有效解决了大规模视频去重中候选检索与匹配精度的权衡问题，具备良好的实际部署效果。

Abstract: The explosive growth of user-generated video content on online platforms is accompanied by the emergence of numerous near-duplicate videos--videos that are identical or highly similar but differ by partial edits. These duplicates degrade user experience and increase storage and bandwidth costs, making large-scale video deduplication a critical task. Existing video deduplication frameworks face a fundamental challenge in retrieving sufficient high-quality candidates under a limited index budget, as well as trade-offs between efficiency and precision. To address these issues, we propose MLT-Dedup, an efficient large-scale online video deduplication framework with Multi-Level representations and spatial-Temporal matching. Our approach employs a Multi-Level Video Encoder (ML-VE) to extract both fine-grained frame-level and sparse clip-level embeddings: sparse embeddings support efficient candidate retrieval, while fine-grained embeddings are loaded for precise pairwise matching. During matching, we introduce DiF-SiM, a Differential Feature-enhanced Similarity Module capable of locating duplicated temporal segments and providing reliable similarity evidence to support policy-driven deduplication decisions. Extensive experiments on a real-world large-scale platform demonstrate that MLT-Dedup reduces online repetition rates by 91% at 90% precision. Furthermore, our sparse retrieval design achieves a 5x increase in indexing capacity, enabling broader candidate coverage in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [89] [Findings of the MAGMaR 2026 Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12295)
*Alexander Martin,Dengjia Zhang,Joel Brogan,Francis Ferraro,Jeremy Gwinnup,Reno Kriz,Teng Long,Kenton Murray,Andrew Yates,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MAGMaR第二届研讨会共享任务的结果，包括视频检索和基于检索视频的接地文章生成两个子任务。检索任务中2支团队提交的17个系统均超越了去年基线；生成任务中4支团队的16个系统均有人工标注为最佳的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动多模态增强生成（通过多模态检索）领域的发展，通过共享任务评估和比较不同系统在视频检索和接地文章生成方面的性能。

Method: 组织共享任务，设置两个子任务：(i) 视频检索；(ii) 基于检索视频的接地文章生成。参赛团队可任选其一或同时参与两个任务。

Result: 检索任务：2支团队提交17个系统，全部超越去年冠军基线；生成任务：4支团队提交16个系统，每个团队至少有一个生成报告被人工标注为最佳。

Conclusion: 共享任务成功展示了多模态检索与生成领域的进展，多个系统在各自任务上表现优异。

Abstract: This overview paper presents the results of the shared task for the second workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via Multimodal Retrieval (MAGMaR). In this shared task participants submitted systems focused on either (i) video retrieval or (ii) grounded generation of articles given retrieved videos. Teams could submit to either task. For the retrieval task, we had 2 participating teams that submitted a total of 17 systems -- all of which beat a baseline derived from the winner of last year's shared task. On the generation side, we had 4 teams submit 16 systems. All teams had at least one generated report that was labeled the best by a human annotator.

</details>


### [90] [Making Foresight Actionable: Repurposing Representation Alignment in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12217)
*Lu Qiu,Yizhuo Li,Yi Chen,Yuying Ge,Yixiao Ge,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGRA方法，通过动作对齐表示学习，解决世界动作模型中视觉重建与动作控制之间的表征不匹配问题，提升机器人操作策略的性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型虽然能生成合理的视觉未来帧，但生成好的视觉结果并不总能保证提取出准确的动作，原因是动作解码器未能关注任务相关的交互区域，且对无关区域的扰动敏感，存在表征不匹配问题。

Method: 提出AGRA（Action-Grounded Representation Alignment），通过将中间视频扩散特征与基础视觉编码器的空间连贯语义表示进行对齐，正则化世界-动作接口，使表征更专注于任务相关的交互区域。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，AGRA改进了目标定位精度和affordance理解能力，使策略对任务无关区域的扰动更加鲁棒，并在分布内性能和分布外泛化上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过动作导向的表征对齐，可以有效弥补视觉重建与动作控制之间的鸿沟，世界模型表征需要更注重动作实用性而非仅追求视觉真实性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising route for robot manipulation by using video generation models to model future scene evolution before producing control actions. However, our empirical observations reveal a phenomenon: generating plausible visual futures does not always guarantee the extraction of accurate actions. To diagnose this failure, we conduct action-head attention analysis and causal interventions. We find that the action decoder fails to focus on task-relevant interaction regions and remains sensitive to perturbations in task-irrelevant areas. This reveals a representation mismatch: hidden states optimized for visual reconstruction are not inherently organized in a form useful for low-level action control. In this paper, we propose AGRA, an Action-Grounded Representation Alignment objective that regularizes the world-action interface by aligning intermediate video diffusion features with spatially coherent semantic representations from a foundation visual encoder. We evaluate AGRA on real-world manipulation tasks. Experiments show that AGRA makes world model representations more action-grounded: by focusing the action decoder on the correct interaction regions, it improves object localization accuracy and affordance understanding, and makes the policy more robust to perturbations in task-irrelevant regions. As a result, AGRA consistently improves both in-distribution performance and out-of-distribution generalization over the baseline world action model.

</details>


### [91] [Adapting Prithvi-EO for Fallow Detection for Food-Water Nexus: ViT-Adapter Necks and Parameter-Efficient Backbone tuning of Geospatial Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12218)
*Sk Muhammad Asif,Orhun Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结合LoRA和混合PEFT两种参数高效微调方案，以及三种颈部设计，用于基于Prithvi-EO地理空间基础模型的休耕地检测。最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter + 单阶段检测头）在mAP@50上达到0.9479，比基线方法提升25.70%，证明轻量级空间先验融合和选择性骨干网络解冻能有效捕捉局部休耕模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 休耕地空间分布对优化粮食-水关系至关重要，但休耕在USDA作物数据层中是低精度类别。现有方法中，地理空间基础模型Prithvi-EO的Vision Transformer骨干网络产生单一空间尺度特征，不适合目标检测头所需的多尺度特征；而现有方法通过缩放单步长token合成多尺度金字塔会牺牲空间异质性，且全骨干微调计算成本过高。

Method: 评估了两种参数高效微调方案（LoRA和混合PEFT）与三种颈部设计（伪多尺度、Lite ViT-Adapter、Full ViT-Adapter）的组合管道，用于休耕地检测。最佳配置采用Lite ViT-Adapter与单阶段检测头，结合DIoU损失。

Result: 最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter + 单阶段头）mAP@50达0.9479；LoRA下ViT-Adapter自由单阶段检测比无适配器的锚点方法提升6.42%；最佳配置比基线无适配器锚点方法提升25.70%。

Conclusion: 轻量级空间先验融合和选择性骨干网络解冻使Prithvi-EO能更有效捕捉局部休耕模式，优于依赖重塑单步长ViT token的方法。

Abstract: Understanding spatial distribution of fallow land is important for optimizing the food-water (FW) nexus, given fallowing's role in crop rotation and water conservation. Fallow is a low accuracy class in USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Geospatial foundation model (GFM), Prithvi-EO has shown strong transferability across computer vision tasks. However, its Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone produces features at a single spatial scale that are ill-suited for the multi-scale features required by object detection heads. Existing approaches synthesise multi-scale pyramids through scaling of single stride tokens, sacrificing spatial heterogeneity, and full backbone fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive for GFMs. We evaluate a fallow detection pipeline combining two parameter-efficient fine tuning (PEFT) schemes: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and a hybrid PEFT, with three neck designs: pseudo multi-scale, Lite ViT-Adapter, and Full ViT-Adapter. Our best configuration, Lite ViT-Adapter with a one-stage head, achieves a mAP@50 of 0.9479 with the Diou loss, suggesting the effectiveness of center-aware localization for irregular fallow field detection. ViT-Adapter free one-stage detection under LoRA improves the adapter-free anchor-based approach by 6.42%, and the best configuration improves baseline adapter-free anchor-based approach by 25.70%. These results demonstrate that lightweight spatial prior fusion and selective backbone unfreezing enable Prithvi-EO to capture local fallow patterns more effectively, outperforming approaches that rely on reshaped single-stride ViT tokens.

</details>


### [92] [An Electric Potential-Augmented Benchmark Dataset for Physics-Guided Image Reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12226)
*Xinqi Zhang,Qiming Ma,Lihui Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含电场势信息的ECT基准数据集，通过COMSOL-MATLAB管道生成，包含20,000个样本和八种激励方式的完整势场图，旨在显式地将潜在物理场信息融入深度学习过程，提升ECT图像重建的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将ECT传感器视为黑箱，直接映射电容与介电常数分布，忽视了作为非线性"软场"效应物理基础的电场势场信息。

Method: 通过COMSOL-MATLAB管道为八电极传感器生成数据集，包含20,000个随机样本（四种典型流型），每个样本除传统电容向量和介电常数分布外，还保留八个激励方式的完整势场图，并提供正问题和逆问题的评估协议。

Result: 在分布内和分布外场景下的综合测试表明，显式加入电场势图显著提升了建模的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式纳入潜在场信息降低了物理规律融入ECT建模的门槛，为未来物理引导的ECT图像重建机器学习建立了标准化基础。

Abstract: While deep learning has significantly advanced image reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography (ECT), most data-driven methods map directly between capacitance and permittivity distribution, treating the sensor as a black box. This overlooks the electric potential field -- the fundamental physical link governing the nonlinear and ill-posed ``soft-field'' effect. To address this, we propose an electric potential-augmented ECT benchmark dataset designed to explicitly integrate latent physics behind ECT into the learning process. Generated via a COMSOL-MATLAB pipeline for an eight-electrode sensor as an example, the dataset comprises 20,000 randomized samples across four typical flow patterns. Crucially, alongside the conventional capacitance vectors and permittivity distributions depicted as images, each sample preserves eight excitation-wise full-field potential maps. Beyond data release, we provide illustrative evaluation protocols for both forward and inverse problems of ECT. Through comprehensive testing on both in-distribution (IID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, we systematically demonstrate how the inclusion of electric potential maps enhances modeling accuracy and robustness. Fundamentally, the explicit inclusion of latent field information significantly lowers the barrier to integrating physical laws into ECT modeling, thereby establishing a standardized foundation for future physics-guided machine learning of ECT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [93] [Damage-TriageFormer: A Foundation-Model Framework for Typology-Based Building Damage Assessment from Mono-Temporal Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12248)
*Yiming Xiao,Yu-Hsuan Ho,Sanjay Thasma,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Damage-TriageFormer，一种基于单张灾后影像的建筑损伤分类模型，不依赖灾前影像，将损伤分为五类（屋顶损伤/结构损伤×部分/全部），在飓风与野火灾害数据上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑损伤评估方法存在两个问题：一是将损伤简化为单一严重程度等级（无/轻微/严重/倒塌），丢失了损伤类型信息；二是大多需要成对的灾前-灾后影像，但在新兴灾害中难以获取。因此需要一种仅依赖单张灾后影像、能区分损伤类型的评估方法。

Method: 以DINOv3 ViT-L为骨干网络，扩展Simple Feature Pyramid实现高分辨率实例池化，设计两阶段门控制损伤头，并引入辅助严重程度回归目标。同时构建DamageTriage-Bench基准数据集，包含五类损伤类型（无损伤、部分屋顶损伤、全部屋顶损伤、部分结构损伤、全部结构坍塌），来源于飓风Michael、飓风Helene和洛杉矶野火灾害的NOAA应急影像。

Result: 验证集macro F1为0.624，测试集macro F1为0.619；无损伤建筑F1为0.91，全部结构坍塌F1为0.84；全部屋顶损伤类别因样本稀少和标签边界模糊仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 仅凭单张灾后影像即可支持可操作的建筑损伤类型评估，无需灾前参考影像，有助于针对性的应急响应和资源分配。

Abstract: Decision-relevant building damage assessment is critical for prioritizing resources and recovery after a disaster, yet most automated methods either flatten damage into a single severity scale (no damage, minor, major, destroyed) or require paired pre- and post-event imagery that is often unavailable for emerging hazards. This paper presents Damage-TriageFormer, a single-image, post-event, footprint-conditioned model that produces a damage typology rather than a severity scale. We contribute: (1) DamageTriage-Bench, a new benchmark built from NOAA Emergency Response Imagery across Hurricane Michael (2018), Hurricane Helene (2024), and the 2025 Los Angeles wildfire complex, with five typology classes that distinguish roof damage from structural damage and, within each, partial from total extent; and (2) Damage-TriageFormer, which extends a DINOv3 ViT-L backbone with a Simple Feature Pyramid for higher-resolution instance pooling, a two-stage gated damage head, and an auxiliary severity-regression objective. Our model achieves macro F1 of 0.624 on validation and 0.619 on a held-out stratified test set, performing strongest where operational triage needs it most, with per-class F1 of 0.91 and 0.84 on undamaged buildings and total structural collapse, respectively. While the rare Total Roof Damage class remains difficult due to its limited examples and an inherently ambiguous label boundary, our results show that single-image post-event imagery can support actionable building damage typing, enabling targeted emergency response and resource allocation without a pre-event reference.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging Day and Night: Unsupervised Cross-Domain Re-Identification with Synergistic Prompt and Prototype Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12258)
*Jiyang Xu,Rui Liu,Hang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督的跨域昼夜行人重识别框架，结合提示学习和原型表示学习，无需人工标注即可实现昼夜身份关联，在无监督设置下达到了与全监督方法相当的Rank-1精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昼夜场景间巨大的视觉外观差异使跨域昼夜行人重识别面临挑战，现有全监督方法依赖昂贵的人工标注且泛化能力有限，因此亟需无监督解决方案。

Method: 提出两阶段训练策略：第一阶段利用视觉-语言模型生成无标注的实例级文本提示，通过实例对齐机制将视觉特征与文本提示映射到统一语义空间；第二阶段构建领域特定原型记忆库，包含域内身份关联模块（增强特征判别性）和跨域原型匹配模块（建立昼夜身份对应关系）。

Result: 在公开基准上，该方法在无监督设置下取得了与最先进全监督方法相当的Rank-1准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的无监督框架通过提示学习与原型表示学习的协同，有效解决了昼夜跨域行人重识别问题，降低了标注成本且性能优异。

Abstract: Cross-domain day-night re-identification (ReID) is fundamentally challenged by the substantial visual appearance discrepancies between daytime and nighttime scenes. Existing fully supervised methods rely heavily on labor-intensive annotations, which are costly and exhibit limited generalization across domains. In this work, we investigate unsupervised day-night ReID and propose a novel framework that synergistically combines prompt learning and prototype-based representation learning to associate identities across domains without requiring manual labels. Our approach follows a progressive two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, we exploit the vision-language model to generate instance-specific textual prompts in an annotation-free manner. We employ an instance-level alignment mechanism to embed visual features and textual prompts into a unified semantic space, aligning unlabeled day/night images with learnable prompts via instance-aware dynamic-bias adaptation. In the second stage, we construct domain-specific prototype memory banks and introduce two complementary modules: i) an intra-domain identity association module to enhance feature discriminability within each domain, and ii) a cross-domain prototype matching module to reliably identify positive and negative prototype pairs, thereby establishing robust identity correspondences across day and night. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method. Under the unsupervised setting, our framework attains Rank-1 accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [95] [VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12263)
*Chunlin Qiu,Ang Li,Tianxiao Huang,Ruilin Gan,Yunjie Ge,Shenyi Zhang,Huayi Duan,Lingchen Zhao,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOID是一种通过操控LDM内在随机性来防御未经授权模仿的框架，通过放大潜在编码错误和抵消目标引导信号实现语义破坏，在不影响视觉质量的前提下将FID从113提升至365，比现有最强防御提升223%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御通过注入欺骗性扰动来防御LDM对个人的未经授权模仿，但这种方法基于一个未经证实的假设：细微扰动能在LDM漫长的生成过程中保持欺骗效果。实际上，模型固有的修复机制会移除这些扰动，导致个体身份在生成的图像中重新出现。

Method: VOID通过两种方式扰动扩散流程：1) 放大潜在编码错误以破坏图像的语义结构；2) 抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型的修复能力。同时将扰动限制在人类不可感知的区域。

Result: 在24种最先进防御方法、10种模仿攻击和5个数据集上的综合评估表明，VOID将平均FID从113提升到365，比现有最强防御提升223%。

Conclusion: VOID实现了前所未有的保护能力，在不牺牲视觉实用性的前提下有效防御未经授权的模仿。

Abstract: While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated.
  We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.

</details>


### [96] [Finding Sparse Subnetworks in One Training Cycle via Progressive Magnitude-Based Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12278)
*Romana Qureshi,Hafida Benhidour,Said Kerrache,Nahlah Aljeraisy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式幅度剪枝方法，作为彩票假设（LTH）迭代剪枝的单周期替代方案。在CIFAR-10和MNIST上对ResNet、VGG、LeNet架构进行实验，结果表明该方法在多种稀疏度下均达到或超越LTH、SNIP、GraSP等基线方法的准确率，为神经网络稀疏化提供了高效的单周期方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩票假设（LTH）的迭代剪枝过程需要多次完整训练周期，计算成本高昂。本文旨在探索一种单周期（single-cycle）的剪枝替代方案，在保持模型性能的同时降低训练开销。

Method: 提出渐进式幅度剪枝（progressive magnitude-based pruning），在训练过程中按照线性调度逐渐增加稀疏度，并基于活跃权重幅度动态更新剪枝掩码。整个过程仅需一次完整训练。

Result: CIFAR-10上，ResNet-18在72.9%稀疏度下达到95.12%准确率（LTH为90.5%）；VGG类似架构在97%稀疏度下达93.13%（SNIP约92.0%）；VGG-19在97.97%稀疏度下达93.44%（GraSP在98%稀疏度为92.19%）。ResNet-18在70-85%稀疏度范围内，准确率与稠密基线差距在0.1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 渐进式幅度剪枝是一种有效的单周期神经网络稀疏化方法，在多种架构和稀疏度设置下均能达到或超越迭代剪枝方法的性能表现。

Abstract: Neural network pruning reduces model size by removing less important parameters while aiming to preserve predictive performance. Although the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (LTH) shows that sparse subnetworks can match dense networks when trained from suitable initializations, its iterative pruning procedure requires multiple complete training cycles. This work evaluates progressive magnitude-based pruning as a single-cycle alternative. The method gradually increases sparsity during training using a linear schedule and updates pruning masks based on active weight magnitudes. We conduct systematic experiments on CIFAR-10 and MNIST across ResNet, VGG-style, and LeNet architectures, comparing the proposed method with representative iterative and initialization-based pruning baselines, including LTH, SNIP, and GraSP. On CIFAR-10, the method achieves 95.12\% accuracy on ResNet-18 at 72.9\% sparsity, compared with 90.5\% reported for LTH. At extreme sparsity, it achieves 93.13\% accuracy on a VGG-like architecture at 97\% sparsity, compared with approximately 92.0\% for SNIP, and 93.44\% accuracy on VGG-19 at 97.97\% sparsity, compared with 92.19\% for GraSP at 98\% sparsity. A sparsity-accuracy analysis on ResNet-18 further shows that accuracy remains within 0.1 percentage points of the dense baseline across 70--85\% sparsity. These results indicate that progressive magnitude-based pruning provides an effective single-cycle approach for neural network sparsification under the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [97] [CellNet -- Localizing Cells using Sparse and Noisy Point Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12286)
*Benjamin Eckhardt,Dmytro Fishman,Stuart Fawke,Andrew Curtis,Bo Fussing,Constantin Pape*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于回归的深度学习算法（CellNet），仅需稀疏点标注即可在相差显微镜图像中检测和计数活细胞，相比零样本方法在低数据场景下表现更优，并已应用于Wellcome Sanger研究所的人类基因组饱和编辑筛选研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型饱和基因组编辑筛选研究中，需要反复大量计数细胞，人工标注成本高且成为瓶颈，亟需一种自动化、高吞吐且标注高效的方法。

Method: 采用基于回归的深度学习计算机视觉算法，仅使用稀疏点注释（快速易获取）进行训练，对相差显微镜图像中的活细胞进行检测和计数。

Result: 与最先进的零样本方法相比，回归式计数在低数据场景下展现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 回归式计数是低数据条件下细胞计数的有前景方案，代码已开源，为人类基因组研究提供了有价值的技术支持。

Abstract: Counting living cells is an important step in many biological research workflows. Our collaborators at the Wellcome Sanger Institute study vital genes in humans via large scale saturation genome editing screening, which requires repeatedly counting cells a great number of times. Computer Vision based automation is crucial for high throughput and resource efficiency. In this work, we develop a regression-based deep learning computer vision algorithm to detect and count cells in phase-contrast microscopy images. To reduce annotation effort, which in practice often becomes a bottleneck, we focus on counting cells only using sparse point annotations, which are fast and easy to acquire. By comparison to state-of-the-art 0-shot methods, we show that regression-based counting is a promising alternative in low data regimes. Through developing methods to automatically count living cells in microscopy images, we contribute to valuable research on the human genome. The code is available at https://github.com/beijn/cellnet.

</details>


### [98] [Bridging the Modality Gap in Forensic Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12294)
*Ricardo González-Gazapo,Annette Morales-González,Yoanna Martínez-Díaz,Heydi Méndez-Vázquez,Milton García-Borroto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个统一的多模态检索框架，结合多模态大语言模型与多种嵌入策略，在四项法医任务（纹身图像检索、基于文本描述的纹身检索、手绘草图纹身检索、法医面部素描人脸检索）中提升了检索精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法医图像检索研究主要集中于多模态检索系统的开发与优化，但缺乏对这些技术在多样化真实场景中法医适用性的系统性评估。

Method: 利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）自动生成结构化的文本描述，结合句子变换器嵌入进行文本比较；同时采用视觉嵌入、文本嵌入及多模态融合策略（融合文本与图像相似度分数）进行检索。

Result: 多模态融合策略持续提升了检索精度与鲁棒性，尤其在视觉信息有限或噪声较大的场景（如草图、部分纹身、碎片化证人描述）中表现显著。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态检索管道具有法医学价值，现代MLLM能够有效支持传统上依赖人工专家分析的法医任务，多模态检索有望成为辅助调查工作流程的有力工具。

Abstract: Automated image retrieval plays an increasingly critical role in modern forensic analysis, supporting investigative workflows that rely on efficient comparison of visual evidence. While prior work has focused primarily on developing and optimizing multimodal retrieval systems, limited attention has been paid to evaluating the forensic applicability of these technologies across diverse real-world scenarios. In this study, we present a unified retrieval framework adapted to four key forensic tasks: (1) tattoo image retrieval given a tattoo query image; (2) tattoo retrieval guided by human-expert textual descriptions, modelling the common situation where a witness verbally describes a tattoo; (3) tattoo retrieval from hand-drawn sketches; and (4) face retrieval from forensic face sketches. Our system leverages a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to automatically generate structured textual descriptions for all queries and gallery images, followed by sentence-transformer embedding for text-based comparison. We evaluate retrieval using visual-only embeddings, text-only embeddings and a multimodal fusion strategy that combines text- and image-based similarity scores derived from state-of-the-art visual feature extractors relevant to each task. The fusion of modalities consistently improves retrieval precision and robustness, especially in scenarios where visual information is limited or noisy (e.g., sketches, partial tattoos, or fragmented witness statements). This work highlights the forensic value of a unified multimodal retrieval pipeline and demonstrates how modern MLLMs can operationalize challenging forensic tasks that traditionally rely on manual expert analysis. Our results position multimodal retrieval as a promising tool for supporting investigative workflows involving tattoos, facial composites, and witness descriptions.

</details>


### [99] [Natural-Language Temporal Grounding in Hour-Long Videos is a Search Problem: A Benchmark and Empirical Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12300)
*Sukmin Seo,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出小时级视频时序定位任务，发现Video-LLM的主要瓶颈在于搜索而非识别，并发布首个开放域小时级基准ExtremeWhenBench。实验表明，所有开放Video-LLM均性能崩溃，而帧级检索基线表现更好；85%的失败归因于搜索问题；检索-定位混合方法相比单一Video-LLM提升6.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序定位研究局限于短视频，小时级长视频的自然语言定位动态尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 发布ExtremeWhenBench基准（2273个查询，194个视频，平均75.7分钟），提出失败分类法分析错误类型，并构建检索-定位混合方法（retrieve-then-ground）。

Result: 所有开放Video-LLM性能崩溃，帧级检索基线表现更优；85%失败归因于搜索；检索-定位混合方法比单一Video-LLM提升6.7倍。

Conclusion: 小时级视频时序定位的核心瓶颈是搜索而非识别，检索-定位混合范式优于单一模型，类似开放域问答中的retrieve-then-read策略。

Abstract: Temporal grounding--returning the interval $[t_s, t_e]$ for a natural-language query over a video--is the language interface to long-form video, yet has been studied on short videos; the dynamics of hour-scale natural-language grounding remain underexplored. We take the position that at hour-scale, the binding constraint is search, not recognition: Video-LLMs are bottlenecked not by localizing a nearby event, but--given a natural-language query--by searching for the relevant region of a long video. To test this, we release ExtremeWhenBench, the first open hour-scale grounding benchmark (2,273 queries over 194 videos, mean 75.7 min, max 9 hr) with an open-form query distribution. Every open Video-LLM collapses while a frame-level retrieval baseline outperforms them; a failure taxonomy attributes 85% of failures to search; and a retrieve-then-ground hybrid recovers 6.7x over the monolithic Video-LLM--mirroring retrieve-then-read in open-domain QA.

</details>


### [100] [From 2D Grids to 1D Tokens: Reforming Shared Representations for Multimodal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12303)
*Yuchen Xian,Yunqiu Xu,Yang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于1D Token接口的多模态图像融合方法，通过选择性Token编辑（STE）在保持局部结构的同时实现全局外观一致性，在多个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于2D特征网格构建共享表示，擅长建模局部结构但难以控制全局外观因素，需要一种能平衡局部细节保留与全局外观一致性的融合方法。

Method: 引入基于冻结预训练图像分词器的紧凑1D Token接口作为全局外观载体，保留2D空间路径用于局部结构恢复；提出选择性Token编辑（STE）机制，稀疏更新/替换少量关键Token来引导全局外观一致性，不改变融合主干架构且无需额外损失函数。

Result: 在四个常用基准数据集上取得最佳整体性能，在全局一致性和局部保真度方面均实现一致的多指标改进。

Conclusion: 1D Token接口结合选择性编辑策略可有效平衡多模态图像融合中局部细节保留与全局外观一致性的矛盾，是一种轻量且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from different modalities into a fused image that preserves rich local details while maintaining globally consistent appearance. Existing approaches build shared representations on 2D feature grids, which excel at modeling local structures but offer limited leverage over image-level global appearance factors. To balance these objectives, we introduce a compact 1D token interface based on a frozen pretrained image tokenizer for modeling non-local appearance/base factors. Rather than using the tokenizer as a reconstruction backbone, our design uses the 1D token space as a global carrier while retaining the 2D spatial pathway for local structure restoration. Specifically, we introduce Selective Token Editing (STE), which sparsely updates/replaces a small set of critical tokens, providing a lightweight mechanism to steer global appearance coherence while keeping the fusion backbone unchanged and avoiding extra losses. Experiments on four commonly used benchmarks show that our method achieves the best overall performance, with consistent, multi-metric improvements in both global coherence and local fidelity. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/1D-Fusion-Project-Page/

</details>


### [101] [Slots, Transitions, Loops: Learning Composable World Models for ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12316)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop-OWM是一种面向对象的环状世界建模架构，通过颜色原型槽、演示条件任务摘要和循环转换模型，在视觉符号层面学习ARC任务的规则，在ARC-1和ARC-2上超越基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多通过语言、代码或符号程序表达ARC规则，但ARC本质上是视觉符号任务，规则表现为网格中对象、颜色、形状和空间关系的过渡。作者希望设计一种直接在视觉符号世界状态上学习可组合规则过渡的方法。

Method: 提出Loop-OWM架构，包含三个核心组件：颜色原型槽（color-prototype slots）用于表示对象特征；演示条件任务摘要（demonstration-conditioned task summaries）用于编码上下文信息；循环转换模型（looped transition model）结合密集传播和槽条件校正，逐步优化状态预测。

Result: 在ARC-1和ARC-2两个基准上，Loop-OWM在参数数量相当或更少的情况下，优于非循环和循环基线模型。

Conclusion: ARC规则不仅可以作为语言描述或搜索到的程序来学习，还可以作为视觉符号世界状态上的过渡来学习，验证了面向对象的世界建模在规则归纳任务中的有效性。

Abstract: ARC tests in-context rule induction: given a few input-output demonstrations, a model must infer the hidden rule and apply it to a new query. While many approaches express ARC rules through language, code, or symbolic programs, ARC itself is visual-symbolic: rules appear as grid transitions over objects, colors, shapes, and spatial relations. We introduce Loop-OWM, an object-centric world-modeling architecture that learns these rules as composable transitions over structured states. It combines color-prototype slots, demonstration-conditioned task summaries, and a looped transition model with dense propagation and slot-conditioned correction. On both ARC-1 and ARC-2, Loop-OWM outperforms non-looped and looped baselines with comparable or fewer parameters. These results suggest that ARC rules can be learned not only as language descriptions or searched programs, but also as transitions over visual-symbolic world states.

</details>


### [102] [Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement for Topology-Aware Circle of Willis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12319)
*Juraj Perić,Marija Habijan,Dario Mužević,Irena Galić,Danilo Babin,Aleksandra Pižurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AC2RUNet，通过双流架构（静态流+动态流）和动态课程学习，从MRA图像中分割Willis环，显著减少血管断裂伪影，提升拓扑连接性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CNN分割Willis环时难以捕捉复杂拓扑约束，导致"血管断裂"伪影问题。

Method: 提出AC2RUNet，采用双流架构：静态流提取不变解剖特征，轻量动态流迭代细化拓扑错误；并引入动态课程学习策略，从高召回几何监督过渡到拓扑感知约束。

Result: 在TopCoW数据集上，Hausdorff距离从9.17mm降至4.72mm，Betti数误差从0.40降至0.19，拓扑连接性显著优于nnU-Net基线，体积Dice保持相当。

Conclusion: AC2RUNet有效解决了CoW分割中的拓扑断裂问题，在保持分割精度的同时大幅提升了拓扑连通性。

Abstract: Segmenting the Circle of Willis (CoW) from Magnetic Resonance Angiography (MRA) is challenging due to complex topology and thin vascular structures that are prone to fragmentation. Standard Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often fail to capture these topological constraints, resulting in "broken vessel" artifacts. To address this, we propose the Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement U-Net (AC2RUNet). Our architecture decouples segmentation into two streams: a Static Stream that extracts invariant anatomical features and a lightweight Dynamic Stream that iteratively refines topological errors over time. We further introduce a dynamic curriculum learning strategy that transitions from high-recall geometric supervision to topology-aware constraints. Validated on the TopCoW dataset, AC2RUNet substantially reduces Hausdorff Distance (4.72 mm vs 9.17 mm) and Betti number errors (0.19 vs 0.40), improving topological connectivity over the nnU-Net baseline while maintaining comparable volumetric Dice.

</details>


### [103] [Echoes of the Prior: A Computational Phenomenology of Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12340)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过可控的3D重建模型突触衰减，将神经网络作为认知代理，可视化遗忘带来的主观混乱体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过艺术化手段可视化遗忘的主观现象学体验，将神经网络不是作为工程工具，而是作为认知代理来模拟记忆衰退过程。

Method: 在前馈3D重建模型中诱导受控的突触衰减，模拟大脑预测先验的侵蚀过程。

Result: 生成了一个交互式装置作品，展示了记忆衰退导致的视觉世界从有序到混乱的退化过程。

Conclusion: 提供了一种神经形态美学的框架，邀请学界探索可视化智能脆弱性的未开发潜力。

Abstract: Memory is not merely the storage of data; it is the scaffolding of reality. When biological memory fades, the world does not simply turn black; it regresses into an unrecognizable chaos. Echoes of the Prior is an interactive installation that attempts to visualize this subjective phenomenology of forgetting. By inducing controlled synaptic decay within a Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction model, we create an artistic analogy for the erosion of the brain's predictive priors. We position the Neural Network not as a tool for engineering, but as a cognitive proxy - a silicon brain whose structural degeneration evokes the disorienting, poetic, and terrifying experience of losing one's grip on the world. Ultimately, we offer this framework as a catalyst, inviting the wider community to explore the uncharted potential of neuromorphic aesthetics in visualizing the fragility of intelligence. Interactive demo see https://decart-4d.github.io/.

</details>


### [104] [Atlas H&E-TME: Scalable AI-Based Tissue Profiling at Expert Pathologist-Level Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12346)
*Kai Standvoss,Miriam Hägele,Rosemarie Krupar,Julika Ribbat-Idel,Jennifer Altschüler,Gerrit Erdmann,Hans Pinckaers,Evelyn Ramberger,Madleen Drinkwitz,Ádám Nárai,Alexander Möllers,Katja Lingelbach,Sebastian Kons,Lukas Hönig,Recepcan Adigüzel,Joana Baião,Alberto Megina Gonzalo,Marius Teodorescu,Marie-Lisa Eich,Paolo Chetta,Shakil Merchant,Verena Aumiller,Simon Schallenberg,Andrew Norgan,Klaus-Robert Müller,Lukas Ruff,Maximilian Alber,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Atlas H&E-TME，一个基于病理基础模型家族的AI系统，能够从H&E全切片图像中预测组织质量、组织区域和细胞类型标签，每个切片提供超过4500个细胞级别的定量指标。通过IHC引导的多病理学家共识协议和超过20万条高置信度病理学家注释的双重验证框架，该系统在多个癌种中匹配或超越了病理学家仅基于H&E的判断能力，将H&E切片转化为可扩展的定量肿瘤微环境分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H&E染色是组织病理学的基石，但其全切片图像的规模化定量分析仍是计算病理学中的核心挑战。现有方法依赖于H&E-only ground truth存在形态模糊性问题，且缺乏可扩展的、基于IHC等模态的更准确参考标准。

Method: 基于Atlas病理基础模型家族，构建预测组织质量、组织区域和细胞类型标签的AI系统；提出IHC引导的多病理学家共识协议作为分子级别参考；在超过20万条高置信度H&E-only病理学家注释上跨8种癌种进行基准测试。

Result: Atlas H&E-TME在IHC引导共识基准上匹配或超越病理学家仅基于H&E的表现，并在广泛的形态学和技术范围内展现出稳定且鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该系统将最普遍的H&E切片转化为可扩展的定量肿瘤微环境分析工具，为转化和临床研究中下一代基于组织的生物标志物奠定了基础。

Abstract: Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining is the cornerstone of histopathology, yet scalable, quantitative analysis of H&E whole-slide images (WSIs) remains a central challenge in computational pathology. We present Atlas H&E-TME, an AI-based system built on the Atlas family of pathology foundation models that predicts tissue quality, tissue region, and cell type labels across multiple cancer types, yielding over 4,500 quantitative readouts per slide at cell-level resolution. A key challenge to validating such systems is overcoming morphological ambiguity inherent to H&E-only ground truth and the limited scalability of more informed references drawing on modalities such as immunohistochemistry (IHC). We address this with a dual validation framework combining biologically grounded depth with technical and morphological breadth. For depth, we propose an IHC-informed multi-pathologist consensus protocol that substantially improves inter-rater agreement over conventional H&E-only annotation. This yields a molecularly grounded reference against which we compare Atlas H&E-TME and pathologists working from H&E alone. For breadth, we benchmark Atlas H&E-TME on over 200,000 high-confidence H&E-only pathologist annotations across 1,500+ cases spanning eight cancer types and their most common metastatic sites, with subtypes covering >90% of clinical cases per cancer type, drawn from 25+ sources and 8+ scanner models. Benchmarked against the IHC-informed consensus, Atlas H&E-TME matches or exceeds pathologist H&E-only performance and generalizes consistently and robustly across this broad morphological and technical scope. In doing so, Atlas H&E-TME turns the H&E slide -- the most ubiquitous data in pathology -- into a scalable, quantitative window into the tumor and its microenvironment, laying a foundation for the next generation of tissue-based biomarkers in translational and clinical research.

</details>


### [105] [DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12368)
*Pengfei Wang,Shihao Wang,Liyi Chen,Zhiyuan Ma,Guowen Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthMaster是一种统一的度量深度估计框架，通过将全景图像分解为重叠透视块、引入对应一致性损失和虚拟投影相机几何先验，解决了透视和全景相机的深度估计统一问题，在13个数据集上实现了最先进的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量深度估计方法通常针对特定相机类型设计，无法在窄视角透视和360°全景两种相机之间泛化，主要面临两个挑战：透视与全景相机之间的几何差异，以及带度量标注的全景训练数据稀缺。

Method: 将全景图像分解为重叠透视块，引入对应一致性损失（CCL）和虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，无需专用网络学习球形失真，保持主干与标准Transformer设计兼容。

Result: 仅使用一个全景数据集混合训练，在13个多样化的数据集上实现了最先进的零样本性能，超越了通用方法和透视/全景领域的专业模型。

Conclusion: 通过统一输入为规范透视表示并利用透视数据集中的度量先验，有效解决了几何差异和数据稀缺问题，实现了跨相机类型的通用度量深度估计。

Abstract: While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.

</details>


### [106] [A Turbo-Inference Strategy for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12371)
*Zhen Zhao,Gang Zhang,Xiaolin Hu,Liang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自上而下实例分割的涡轮推理策略(Turbo-Learning)，通过设计涡轮检测头和涡轮分割头，在检测和分割任务之间形成闭环交互，无需重新训练即可同时提升两项任务的精度，但会带来一定的计算开销增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自上而下实例分割方法遵循"先检测后分割"范式，检测精度直接影响分割性能，但很少有研究探索实例分割任务对目标检测的反向促进作用，缺乏任务间的互补信息利用。

Method: 提出涡轮推理策略，设计两个模块：(1) 涡轮检测头 - 利用分割信息增强检测；(2) 涡轮分割头 - 利用检测信息增强分割。两个模块形成闭环，迭代交互检测与分割结果，且无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在增加一定计算成本的前提下，显著提升了检测和分割的精度。

Conclusion: 该方法在预测精度和推理速度之间取得了良好的平衡，开源代码可供复现。

Abstract: Object detection and instance segmentation tasks are closely related. Existing top-down instance segmentation methods usually follow a detect-then-segment paradigm, where an initial detector is used to recognize and localize objects with bounding boxes, followed by the segmentation of an instance mask within each bounding box. In such methods, the detection accuracy directly influences the subsequent segmentation performance. However, previous research has seldom explored the impact of the instance segmentation task on object detection. In this paper, we present a turbo-inference strategy for the top-down methods that leverages the complementary information between detection and segmentation tasks iteratively. Specifically we design two modules: turbo-detection head and turbo-segmentation head, which facilitate communication between the tasks. The two modules form a closed loop that interlaces the detection and segmentation results without retraining the model. Comprehensive experiments on the COCO, iFLYTEK, and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method substantially enhances both detection and segmentation accuracies with a certain increase in computational cost. The proposed method represents a tradeoff between prediction accuracy and inference speed. Codes are available at https://github.com/zhaozhen2333/Turbo-Learning.git.

</details>


### [107] [Illumination-Robust Camera-Based Heart-Rate Estimation for Physiological Sensing in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12378)
*Zhi Wei Xu,Torbjörn E. M. Nordling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的时空变换器框架，用于在光照变化条件下从RGB摄像头进行远程心率估计，结合3D人脸对齐、光照增强、残差时序标准化和混合时频监督，在自建数据集上实现了0.79 bpm的MAE和0.982的HR相关性，相比PhysFormer基线将MAE降低了93.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积描记术（rPPG）是服务、社交和辅助机器人在日常环境中与人类交互时重要的非接触心率感知技术，但光照变化是其鲁棒部署的主要障碍。

Method: 提出端到端时空变换器框架，集成PRNet 3D人脸对齐、剪辑级光照增强、残差时序标准化模块（RTSM）以及受控混合时频监督。训练目标结合Soft-Shifted Pearson波形损失和频谱KL散度损失，通过权重β控制频域心率引导的贡献。

Result: 在覆盖三种光照级别的静态全级别混合协议上，β=5时取得最佳结果：HR MAE为0.79 bpm，HR相关性为0.982。相比PhysFormer基线，HR MAE降低93.6%，HR相关性从0.088提升至0.982。

Conclusion: 所提方法在光照变化条件下实现了高精度、高鲁棒性的远程心率估计，使rPPG在机器人视觉系统中的应用成为可能。

Abstract: Physiological awareness is important for service, social, and assistive robots that interact with humans in everyday environments. Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate (HR) estimation from an RGB camera, making it a promising sensing modality for robot-mounted vision systems. However, illumination variation remains a major barrier to robust deployment. This paper presents an end-to-end spatial-temporal transformer framework for remote HR estimation on a new dataset with varied illumination. Our estimator integrates PRNet-based 3D face alignment, clip-level illumination augmentation, the Residual Temporal Standardization Module, and controlled hybrid temporal-frequency supervision. The training objective combines a Soft-Shifted Pearson waveform loss with a spectral Kullback-Leibler divergence loss, where a tuned weight ($\mathbfβ$) controls the contribution of frequency-domain heart-rate guidance. Experiments on a static all-level mix protocol covering three illumination levels show that $\mathbfβ=5$ provides the strongest result among the tested beta settings, achieving a best-run HR mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.79 bpm and an HR correlation of 0.982. Compared with the PhysFormer baseline evaluated on our dataset, our estimator reduces HR MAE by 93.6 %, while increasing HR correlation from 0.088 to 0.982, making it usable when illumination varies.

</details>


### [108] [VLGA: Vision-Language-Geometry-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12396)
*Jin Yao,Dhruva Dixith Kurra,Tom Lampo,Zezhou Cheng,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLGA是首个将密集3D几何作为第四模态的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过逐像素点图回归监督，在自动驾驶开放环和闭环评估中均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽能理解场景，但难以在密集3D世界中 grounding 动作，已有方法要么使用冻结的3D特征缺乏监督目标，要么使用稀疏的边界框和地图损失缺乏密集空间信号。

Method: 将几何作为第四模态（与视觉、语言、动作并列），通过专用专家模块和逐像素点图回归损失（以LiDAR为监督）进行训练。

Result: 在nuScenes开放环评估中，平均L2误差0.50m，3秒碰撞率0.18%；在Bench2Drive闭环评估中，驾驶得分79.08，比最强前代VLA高0.71。

Conclusion: VLGA通过密集几何监督显著提升了VLA模型在自动驾驶中的3D动作接地能力，在开放环和闭环任务中均达到最优性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can describe scenes and reason about them in language, yet still struggle to ground their actions in the dense 3D world around them. Existing approaches either inject features from a frozen 3D foundation model without an objective that ensures the policy uses them, or constrain geometry with sparse box and map losses that provide no dense spatial signal. We introduce VLGA, the first vision-language-action model supervised to reconstruct the dense 3D world it drives through. VLGA introduces geometry as a fourth modality alongside vision, language, and action through a dedicated expert supervised by a per-pixel pointmap regression loss against LiDAR. Extensive experiments conducted on challenging nuScenes and Bench2Drive datasets for open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, respectively, show the superiority of VLGA over counterpart VLA methods. In particular, on open-loop nuScenes, VLGA sets a new state of the art among VLA methods without ego status, with the lowest L2 (0.50\,m average) and 3-second collision rate (0.18\%). On closed-loop Bench2Drive, VLGA attains the state-of-the-art driving score of 79.08, +0.71 over the strongest prior VLA, at comparable efficiency and comfort.

</details>


### [109] [How Seemingly Inconsequential Design Choices Dictate Performance of LLMs in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12407)
*Kian R. Weihrauch,Thomas A. Buckley,William Lotter,Arjun K. Manrai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验发现，之前研究中通用大语言模型在病理全切片图像任务上表现差，主要是因为输入配置（如补丁大小、放大倍数等）选择不当。通过优化输入配置，GPT-5在癌症分类任务上从15.1%提升至39.5%，表明领域专用模型与通用LLM之间的差距被高估了。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将通用大语言模型作为专用病理模型的基线，但通用LLM在病理WSI任务上一直表现不佳，这强化了"领域专用训练或架构适配是必要"的认知。作者怀疑这种差距可能源于输入配置选择不当，而非模型本身的能力差距。

Method: 对四个输入设计因素（推理模式、补丁大小、放大倍数、补丁数量）进行系统析因分析，在MultiPathQA基准上测试GPT-5等模型，并验证结果在CPTAC队列和另外两个模型上的泛化性。

Result: 单一平衡配置（大补丁、低放大倍数、联合处理）使GPT-5在TCGA癌症分类从15.1%提升至39.5%，GTEx器官分类从38.1%提升至62.9%。每任务优化进一步分别提升至43.9%和71.6%。该配置泛化到其他模型和CPTAC队列，Gemini 3 Flash提升23.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 先前研究高估了专用模型与通用LLM之间的差距，通用LLM在病理WSI任务上有更大潜力，关键在于选择合适的输入配置。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are routinely used as baselines when evaluating specialized pathology models on whole-slide images (WSIs). Because WSIs exceed contemporary model context limits, LLM baselines routinely use small, high-magnification patches processed independently via majority voting, without systematic evaluation of seemingly inconsequential design choices such as patch size, patch count, and magnification. Generalist LLMs have consistently underperformed specialized systems, reinforcing the perception that domain-specific training or architectural adaptation is necessary for pathology tasks involving WSIs. Here, we conduct a systematic factorial analysis of four input design factors: inference mode, patch size, magnification, and patch count. We demonstrate that prior studies have overstated the gap between specialized models and general-purpose LLMs by choosing non-optimized input configurations. On the MultiPathQA benchmark, switching to a single balanced configuration (large patches at lower magnification, processed jointly) raises GPT-5 from 15.1% to 39.5% on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and from 38.1% to 62.9% on organ classification (GTEx). Per-task optimization yields further gains up to 43.9% (TCGA) and 71.6% (GTEx). The same configuration generalizes to two other models and to a fully held-out CPTAC cohort, where it improves Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without any task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [110] [Reroute, Don't Remove: Recoverable Visual Token Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12412)
*Cheng-Yu Yang,Shao-Yuan Lo,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reroute提出了一种可恢复的路由机制替代不可逆的视觉token剪枝，在保持计算效率的同时提升了VLM的细粒度定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法采用"评分-移除"的不可逆策略，但视觉token的重要性在解码器不同层会动态变化，早期被丢弃的token可能在后续层变得重要，导致细粒度定位任务性能下降。

Method: Reroute是一种免训练的即插即用方法。在每一路由阶段，选中的视觉token通过解码器块处理，而被延迟的token跳过当前阶段并在下一路由决策时重新进入候选池，实现可恢复的路由而非永久丢弃。

Result: 在FastV、PDrop和Nüwa变体（基于LLaVA-1.5和Qwen骨干网络）上，Reroute在激进token缩减下提升了定位能力，同时保持通用VQA性能。

Conclusion: VLM的token缩减不应仅被视为不可逆的剪枝，而应看作可恢复的路由问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) project images into hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, making decoder inference expensive in both attention computation and KV-cache memory. Existing visual-token reduction methods largely follow a rank-and-remove paradigm: they score visual tokens, keep a compact subset, and permanently discard the rest. We show that this irreversible action is fragile because visual-token importance changes across decoder depth; tokens ranked low at one stage may become relevant in later layers, especially for grounding-sensitive queries. We propose Reroute, a training-free plug-in that replaces removal with recoverable routing. At each routing stage, selected vision tokens pass through decoder blocks, while deferred tokens bypass the stage and re-enter the candidate pool at the next routing decision. Reroute reuses existing attention-score ranking rules and stage-wise schedules, preserving the theoretical TFLOPs and KV-cache budget class of the pruning method it augments. Across FastV, PDrop, and Nüwa variants on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones, reroute improves grounding under aggressive token reduction while maintaining general VQA performance. These results suggest that VLM token reduction should not be viewed only as irreversible pruning, but also as recoverable routing. The code can be found here: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [PoQ-Judge: A Multi-Architecture Evaluation Framework for Cost-Aware Proof-of-Quality in Decentralized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11196)
*Arther Tian,Alex Ding,Frank Chen,Simon Wu,Aaron Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PoQ-Judge框架，训练专用裁判模型在去中心化LLM推理网络中实现无参考的质量评估。最佳模型（DeBERTa）在Pearson相关性上达到0.747，级联评估可降低72.7%成本，且无需参考答案即可匹配最优参考评估器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化LLM推理网络需要轻量级、无参考的质量评估机制用于PoQ（质量证明），现有方法依赖参考答案，难以在去中心化场景中应用。

Method: 研究了三种架构（TextCNN、MiniLM交叉编码器、DeBERTa），采用两阶段训练（UltraFeedback + GPT标注的域内数据），并引入在线校准和级联评估策略。

Result: 最佳模型Pearson相关性达0.747，级联评估降低成本72.7%且质量损失小；无参考场景下相关性0.645，匹配最优有参考评估器；QA任务表现优于摘要任务。

Conclusion: PoQ-Judge有效实现了去中心化场景下的无参考质量评估，主要瓶颈在于代理质量（proxy quality）而非模型本身，尤其在摘要任务上仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Decentralized LLM inference networks need lightweight, reference-free quality evaluation for Proof of Quality (PoQ). We present PoQ-Judge, a framework that trains dedicated judge models to score query-output pairs without ground-truth references. We study three architectures across the quality-cost tradeoff: a TextCNN judge, a MiniLM cross-encoder, and a DeBERTa judge. Using two-stage training on UltraFeedback plus GPT-labeled in-domain data, the best model reaches 0.747 Pearson correlation with the ground-truth proxy on a held-out test set, outperforming reference-based evaluators from prior work. As a reference-free component in composite scoring, it achieves 0.645 Pearson correlation, matching the best single reference-based evaluator while removing the need for reference answers. We also show that online calibration identifies semantic quality as the dominant dimension and that cascade evaluation reduces cost by 72.7 percent with only modest quality loss. Results are much stronger on QA than summarization, pointing to proxy quality as the main remaining limitation.

</details>


### [112] [The Structural Attention Tax: How Retrieval Format Hijacks In-Context Learning Independent of Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11198)
*Yuqi Zhang,Di Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现知识图谱三元组格式会通过结构性注意力偏差（结构注意力税）吸引2-3倍于自然语言文本的注意力，导致演示示例注意力被压缩达42%；通过形式化分解注意力的语义与结构成分，提出两轴优化框架，并验证了源任务对齐对RAG-ICL性能的主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统关注注入内容的语义相关性，但忽略了内容格式本身对模型注意力分布的独立扭曲效应。作者旨在识别并形式化这种"结构注意力税"现象。

Method: 形式化分解注意力为语义与结构成分（Eq.2），推导压缩界（Proposition 1）；跨两个模型家族（Mistral-7B, LLaMA-3-8B）和三个QA基准进行实验；提出五种结构感知缓解策略。

Result: KG三元组每token注意力约0.70 vs 自然语言约0.25；演示注意力压缩达42%；源任务对齐主导性能（BM25在HotpotQA上58-62% vs ConceptNet的25-27%），差距远大于不同门控策略（≤2pp）。

Conclusion: RAG-ICL优化存在两个正交轴：语义轴（优化检索质量）和结构轴（减少格式驱动的注意力捕获）；源任务对齐是关键因素，格式扁平化（S3）策略得到验证有效。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems inject external knowledge to improve LLM outputs, yet the format of injected content -- distinct from its semantic relevance -- can independently distort the model's attention distribution. We identify and formalise a phenomenon we term the structural attention tax: knowledge graph (KG) triples, due to their relational delimiters and repeated slot patterns, capture 2-3x more attention per token than semantically equivalent natural-language text ($\hat{o}$(KG) $\approx$ 0.70 vs. $\hat{o}$(neutral) $\approx$ 0.25), compressing demonstration attention by up to 42% -- regardless of whether the triples are relevant or noise. We develop a formal framework decomposing attention scores into semantic and structural components (Eq. 2), derive a compression bound (Proposition 1) connecting token-level format bias to demonstration attention loss, and show that the structural term governs how much attention is diverted while the semantic term governs whether this helps or hurts. This decoupling reveals two orthogonal axes for improving retrieval-augmented ICL: optimising retrieval quality (semantic axis) and reducing format-driven attention capture (structural axis). Empirically, across two model families (Mistral-7B, LLaMA-3-8B) and three QA benchmarks, we observe that source-task alignment dominates: task-matched BM25 retrieval achieves 58-62% on HotpotQA vs. ConceptNet's 25-27%, a >30 pp gap that dwarfs all gating strategies ($\leq$2 pp). We derive five structure-aware mitigation strategies from the framework, ranging from zero-cost prompt modifications to training-time regularisation; format flattening (S3) is validated by both accuracy and attention-level evidence from a verbalized-triple control, while structural dispersal (S1) yields mixed results that illuminate the challenges of format-level intervention.

</details>


### [113] [NightFeats @ MMU-RAGent NeurIPS 2025: A Context-Optimized Multi-Agent RAG System for the Text-to-Text Track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11199)
*Quentin Fever,Naziha Aslam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NightFeats是一个结构化多智能体RAG系统，在NeurIPS 2025比赛中获最佳动态评估奖，通过检索-策展-组合三阶段流水线，引入时序语义重排序、矛盾消解和引用保留架构，超越Claude-SonnetV2等商业基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有RAG系统过于追求基准分数最大化而忽视可解释性和证据可验证性的问题，提出一个强调架构透明性和证据基础的RAG流水线。

Method: 采用三阶段协调流水线：(1) 检索阶段；(2) 策展阶段（含时序语义重排序和有界矛盾消解）；(3) 组合阶段（引用保留式组合）。各阶段由显式中间表示和交接合约驱动，受Agentic Context Engineering (ACE)启发。

Result: 在LLM-as-a-Judge和人类Likert评估中超越Claude-SonnetV2和Nova-Pro等专有基线，获得最佳动态评估奖。

Conclusion: 架构透明性和可验证的证据基础比单纯优化自动相似度指标更符合人类偏好，为RAG系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present NightFeats, a structured multi-agent retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system submitted to the MMU-RAGent competition at NeurIPS 2025, where it was awarded Best Dynamic Evaluation in the text-to-text track. Rather than targeting benchmark maximization, this work proposes a principled pipeline that decomposes knowledge synthesis into three coordinated phases: retrieval, curation, and composition, each governed by explicit intermediate representations and handoff contracts. Inspired by Agentic Context Engineering (ACE), the system introduces temporal-semantic reranking, bounded contradiction reconciliation, and citation-preserving composition as core architectural primitives. Competition results show that NightFeats surpasses proprietary baselines including Claude-SonnetV2 and Nova-Pro on LLM-as-a-Judge and Human Likert evaluations, confirming that architectural transparency and verifiable evidence grounding are better aligned with human preferences than systems optimizing narrowly for automatic similarity metrics.

</details>


### [114] [Detecting AI-Generated Content on Social Media with Multi-modal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11200)
*Chenyang Yang,Shen Yan,Yibo Yang,Litao Hu,Yuchen Liu,Yuan Zeng,Hanchao Yu,Yinan Zhu,Sumedha Singla,Brian Vanover,Huijun Qian,Zihao Wang,Fujun Liu,Aashu Singh,Jianyu Wang,Xuewen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态视觉-语言模型 pipeline，用于检测和解释社交媒体上的 AI 生成内容（AIGC），在公开基准和内部数据集上均达到 SOTA 性能，并成功部署于真实社交平台提升用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AIGC 检测方法存在泛化能力差、依赖单一模态、缺乏可解释性等问题，无法应对社交媒体上日益泛滥的 AI 生成虚假内容（如 spam、 misinformation、 fraud）。

Method: 持续收集多模态社交媒体数据，训练紧凑型视觉-语言模型，同时进行 AIGC 检测和生成可解释的文本说明。

Result: 在公开基准上达到 SOTA 检测性能；在内部跨平台社交媒体数据集上检测和解释能力均表现鲁棒；实际部署后对用户参与度有积极影响。

Conclusion: 证明了在动态真实社交媒体环境中进行有效 AIGC 检测是可行的，且能带来正向的下游业务收益。

Abstract: Generative AI has enabled the creation of photorealistic images and videos that are increasingly disseminated on social media, often used for spam, misinformation, manipulation, and fraud. Existing AI-generated content (AIGC) detection methods face challenges including poor generalization to new generation models, reliance on single modalities, and lack of interpretable explanations. We present our pipeline that mitigates these issues by continuously curating diverse multi-modal social media data and training a compact vision-language model for detection and explanation. Our model achieves state-of-the-art detection performance on public benchmarks and demonstrates robust detection and explanation capabilities on internal social media datasets across multiple platforms.
